   1++6EE1              ; ============================================================================
   2++6EE1              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6EE1              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6EE1              ;
   5++6EE1              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6EE1              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6EE1              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6EE1              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6EE1              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6EE1              ; ============================================================================
  11++6EE1              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6EE1              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6EE1              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6EE1              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6EE1              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6EE1              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6EE1
  18++6EE1              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6EE1                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6EE1 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6EE4 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6EE7 B7                   or      a
  23++6EE8 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6EEA C2 29 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6EED
  26++6EED                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6EED 21 94 94             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6EF0 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6EF3 B7                   or      a
  30++6EF4 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6EF6 DA 29 6F             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6EF9 CA 29 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6EFC 22 43 6F             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6EFF 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6F00 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6F02 D2 29 6F             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6F05
  38++6F05 21 94 94             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6F08 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6F0B B7                   or      a
  41++6F0C ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6F0E DA 17 6F             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6F11 CA 17 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6F14 C3 29 6F             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6F17              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6F17                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6F17 3A F7 74             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6F1A FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6F1C C2 29 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6F1F 3A F4 74             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6F22 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6F24 C2 29 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6F27 B7                   or      a
  54++6F28 C9                   ret
  55++6F29
  56++6F29              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6F29 37                   scf
  58++6F2A C9                   ret
  59++6F2B
  60++6F2B              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6F2B              p127_layout_report:
  62++6F2B 21 45 6F             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6F2E CD 1B 63             call    p127_puts
  64++6F31 3A 44 6F             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6F34 CD 37 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6F37 3A 43 6F             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6F3A CD 37 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6F3D 21 61 6F             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6F40 C3 1B 63             jp      p127_puts
  70++6F43
  71++6F43              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6F43 00 00                dw      0
  73++6F45              p127_msg_kernel_size:
  74++6F45 5B 50 31 32          db      "[P127] KERNEL IMAGE SIZE=0x",0
  74++6F49 37 5D 20 4B
  74++6F4D 45 52 4E 45
  74++6F51 4C 20 49 4D
  74++6F55 41 47 45 20
  74++6F59 53 49 5A 45
  74++6F5D 3D 30 78 00
  75++6F61              p127_msg_kernel_size_tail:
  76++6F61 20 42 59 54          db      " BYTES; P5/P2 BOUNDS PASS",13,10,0
  76++6F65 45 53 3B 20

 467+ 6F7D                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6F7D              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F7D              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6F7D              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6F7D              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6F7D              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6F7D              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6F7D              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6F7D              ; ============================================================================
   9++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6F7D
  13++6F7D              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6F7D              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6F7D              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6F7D              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6F7D              p127_resident_start:
  18++6F7D
  19++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6F7D              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6F7D
  26++6F7D              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6F7D              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6F7D              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6F7D              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6F7D              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6F7D 32 07 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6F80 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6F81 32 08 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6F84 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6F85 32 09 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6F88 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6F89 32 0A 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6F8C
  39++6F8C 3A 07 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6F8F FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6F91 C2 BB 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6F94 3A 08 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6F97 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6F99 C2 BB 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6F9C 3A 09 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6F9F FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6FA1 C2 BB 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6FA4 3A 0A 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6FA7 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6FA9 CA BB 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6FAC                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6FAC 3A 07 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6FAF FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6FB1 CA BB 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6FB4 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6FB6 CA BB 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6FB9 B7                   or      a
  58++6FBA C9                   ret
  59++6FBB
  60++6FBB              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6FBB 37                   scf
  62++6FBC C9                   ret
  63++6FBD
  64++6FBD              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6FBD              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6FBD              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6FBD 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6FBF 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6FC1 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6FC3 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6FC5 CD 7D 6F             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6FC8 DA 05 70             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6FCB
  74++6FCB 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6FCD 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6FCF 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6FD1 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6FD3 CD 7D 6F             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6FD6 D2 05 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6FD9
  81++6FD9 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6FDB 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6FDD 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6FDF 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6FE1 CD 7D 6F             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6FE4 D2 05 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6FE7
  88++6FE7 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6FE9 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6FEB 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6FED 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6FEF CD 7D 6F             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6FF2 D2 05 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6FF5
  95++6FF5 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6FF7 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6FF9 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6FFB 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6FFD CD 7D 6F             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++7000 D2 05 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++7003 B7                   or      a
 102++7004 C9                   ret
 103++7005
 104++7005              p127_resident_test_fail:
 105++7005 37                   scf
 106++7006 C9                   ret
 107++7007
 108++7007              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++7007 00                   db      0
 110++7008              p127_resident_arg_code:
 111++7008 00                   db      0
 112++7009              p127_resident_arg_stack:
 113++7009 00                   db      0
 114++700A              p127_resident_arg_flags:
 115++700A 00                   db      0
 116++700B
 117++700B
 118++700B              ; W3 ownership / UI quiesce state machine. Software only; no page OUT.
 119++700B              P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE EQU 1
 120++700B              P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED EQU 2
 121++700B              P127_W3_OWNER_UI EQU 1
 122++700B              P127_W3_OWNER_NONE EQU 0
 123++700B              P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF EQU 2
 124++700B
 125++700B              p127_w3_init:
 126++700B 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 127++700D 32 A8 71             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 128++7010 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 129++7012 32 A9 71             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 130++7015 AF                   xor     a
 131++7016 32 AA 71             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 132++7019 B7                   or      a
 133++701A C9                   ret
 134++701B
 135++701B              p127_w3_ui_enter:
 136++701B 3A A8 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 137++701E FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 138++7020 20 79                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 139++7022 3A A9 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 140++7025 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 141++7027 20 72                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 142++7029 3A AA 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 143++702C B7                   or      a
 144++702D 20 6C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 145++702F 3E 01                ld      a,1
 146++7031 32 AA 71             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 147++7034 B7                   or      a
 148++7035 C9                   ret
 149++7036
 150++7036              p127_w3_ui_leave:
 151++7036 AF                   xor     a
 152++7037 32 AA 71             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 153++703A C9                   ret
 154++703B
 155++703B              p127_w3_quiesce:
 156++703B 3A A8 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 157++703E FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 158++7040 20 59                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 159++7042 3A A9 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 160++7045 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 161++7047 20 52                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 162++7049 3A AA 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 163++704C B7                   or      a
 164++704D 20 4C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 165++704F 3E 02                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 166++7051 32 A8 71             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 167++7054 AF                   xor     a
 168++7055 32 A9 71             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 169++7058 B7                   or      a
 170++7059 C9                   ret
 171++705A
 172++705A              p127_w3_handoff_acquire:
 173++705A 3A A8 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 174++705D FE 02                cp      P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 175++705F 20 3A                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 176++7061 3A A9 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 177++7064 B7                   or      a
 178++7065 20 34                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 179++7067 3E 02                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF
 180++7069 32 A9 71             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 181++706C B7                   or      a
 182++706D C9                   ret
 183++706E

 185++706E 3A A8 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 186++7071 FE 02                cp      P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 187++7073 20 26                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject

 189++7078 FE 02                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF

 348++71AE              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++71AE              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++71AE              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++71AE              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++71AE              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++71AE              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++71AE              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++71AE              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++71AE              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:
 357++71AE F3                   di
 358++71AF                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++71AF 21 7D 6F             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++71B2 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++71B5 B7                   or      a
 362++71B6 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++71B8 DA FB 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++71BB
 365++71BB 21 FD 71             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++71BE 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++71C1 B7                   or      a
 368++71C2 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++71C4 D2 FB 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++71C7
 371++71C7                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.
 372++71C7 21 A8 71             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 373++71CA 11 7D 6F             ld      de,p127_resident_start
 374++71CD B7                   or      a
 375++71CE ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++71D0 DA FB 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++71D3

 379++71D6 11 FD 71             ld      de,p127_resident_end
 380++71D9 B7                   or      a
 381++71DA ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++71DC D2 FB 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++71DF
 384++71DF                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++71DF                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++71DF 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
 387++71E2 39                   add     hl,sp
 388++71E3 11 00 B0             ld      de,0B000h
 389++71E6 B7                   or      a
 390++71E7 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 391++71E9 DA FB 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 392++71EC

 395++71F0 11 00 C0             ld      de,0C000h
 396++71F3 B7                   or      a
 397++71F4 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 398++71F6 D2 FB 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 399++71F9
 400++71F9 B7                   or      a
 401++71FA C9                   ret

   1++71FD              ; ============================================================================
   2++71FD              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++71FD              ; Module version: 0.4.4
   4++71FD              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++71FD              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++71FD              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++71FD              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++71FD              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++71FD              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++71FD              ; ============================================================================
  11++71FD              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++71FD              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++71FD              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 4

  16++71FD              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++71FD              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1

  74++724D              ; Reserve ATM video RAM used by the complete Project112 mode set.
  75++724D              ; Project112 ATM710: ZX uses page 5 or 7; ATM640/EGA/Text use page
  76++724D              ; pairs 5+1 or 7+3. Reserve the union so any supported mode can be selected.

   2++7572              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator

   4++7572              ; ABI:

   6++7572              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++7572              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL

 470+ 768C                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_arena.asm"
# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++768C              ; ============================================================================
   2++768C              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++768C              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++768C              ;
   5++768C              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++768C              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++768C              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++768C              ;
   9++768C              ; API:
  10++768C              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++768C              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++768C              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++768C              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++768C              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++768C              ;
  16++768C              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++768C              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++768C              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.

   1++77EA              ; ============================================================================
   2++77EA              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++77EA              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++77EA              ;
   5++77EA              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++77EA              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++77EA              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++77EA              ;
   9++77EA              ; API:
  10++77EA              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++77EA              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++77EA              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++77EA              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++77EA              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++77EA              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++77EA              ; ============================================================================
  17++77EA              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++77EA              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++77EA              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++77EA              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++77EA              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++77EA              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh

   1++7C6B              ; ============================================================================
   2++7C6B              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7C6B              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7C6B              ;
   5++7C6B              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7C6B              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7C6B              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7C6B              ;
   9++7C6B              ; API inputs:
  10++7C6B              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7C6B              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7C6B              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7C6B              ; Outputs:
  14++7C6B              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7C6B              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7C6B              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7C6B              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7C6B              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7C6B              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7C6B              ; ============================================================================
  21++7C6B              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7C6B              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7C6B              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7C6B              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7C6B              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7C6B
  27++7C6B              p127_as_copy:
  28++7C6B AF                   xor     a

  30++7C6F 32 66 81             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed),a
  31++7C72 32 67 81             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+1),a
  32++7C75 32 68 81             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+2),a
  33++7C78 32 69 81             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+3),a
  34++7C7B
  35++7C7B                      ; Validate source and destination completely before taking W3.
  36++7C7B 21 5A 81             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source
  37++7C7E 11 57 7C             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  38++7C81 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  39++7C84 ED B0                ldir
  40++7C86 21 62 81             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_length

  44++7C91 CD 8B 7A             call    p127_as_span_check
  45++7C94 DA 7D 7E             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject

  52++7CA5 DA 7D 7E             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject

 311++7E91              ; IN HL=left uint32 pointer, DE=right uint32 pointer.
 312++7E91              ; OUT carry if left<right; Z if equal. Preserves BC.
 313++7E91              p127_as_copy_compare_u32:
 314++7E91 C5                   push    bc

 335++7EAB BE                   cp      (hl)
 336++7EAC              p127_as_copy_compare_done:
 337++7EAC C1                   pop     bc
 338++7EAD C9                   ret
 339++7EAE
 340++7EAE              ; IN HL=14-bit page offset. Reduce current chunk if fewer than 64 bytes

 177++8319              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++8319              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++8319              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++8319              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++8319              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++8319              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++8319              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++8319              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).

   1++850F              ; ============================================================================
   2++850F              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++850F              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++850F              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++850F              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++850F              ; ============================================================================
   7++850F              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++850F              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++850F              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++850F
  11++850F              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++850F              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++850F              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++850F              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++850F              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++850F              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++850F              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++850F
  19++850F              ; IN: none.
  20++850F              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++850F              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++850F              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++850F              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++850F              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++850F              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++850F              p127_handoff_stack_probe:

   4++85AA              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.

   1++8AF7              ; ============================================================================
   2++8AF7              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8AF7              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8AF7              ;
   5++8AF7              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8AF7              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8AF7              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8AF7              ;
   9++8AF7              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8AF7              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8AF7              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8AF7              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8AF7              ;
  14++8AF7              ; API:
  15++8AF7              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8AF7              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8AF7              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8AF7              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8AF7              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8AF7              ;
  21++8AF7              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8AF7              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8AF7              ; is assumed.
  24++8AF7              ; ============================================================================
  25++8AF7              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8AF7              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8AF7              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8AF7
  29++8AF7              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8AF7              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8AF7              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8AF7
  33++8AF7              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8AF7 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8AF9 32 55 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8AFC C9                   ret
  37++8AFD
  38++8AFD              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8AFD              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8AFD              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8AFD              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8AFD C5                   push    bc
  43++8AFE 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8AFF 3A 55 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8B02 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8B04 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8B05 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8B06 E6 01                and     1
  49++8B08 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8B0A 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8B0B F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8B0D 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8B0F              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8B0F 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8B10              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8B10 32 55 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8B13 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8B15 C1                   pop     bc
  59++8B16 C9                   ret
  60++8B17
  61++8B17              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8B17              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8B17 3A 55 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8B1A EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8B1C 32 55 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8B1F D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8B21 C9                   ret
  68++8B22
  69++8B22              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8B22              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8B22 3A 55 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8B25 E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8B27 32 55 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8B2A D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8B2C C9                   ret
  76++8B2D
  77++8B2D              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8B2D 3A 55 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8B30 C9                   ret
  80++8B31
  81++8B31
  82++8B31              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8B31              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8B31              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8B31              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8B31              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8B31 F5                   push    af
  88++8B32 C5                   push    bc
  89++8B33 D5                   push    de
  90++8B34 E5                   push    hl
  91++8B35 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8B37 CD F7 8A             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8B3A 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8B3D              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8B3D CD 17 8B             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8B40 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8B42              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8B42 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8B44 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8B46              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8B46 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8B48 1B                   dec     de
 103++8B49 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8B4A B3                   or      e
 105++8B4B 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8B4D CD 22 8B             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8B50 E1                   pop     hl
 108++8B51 D1                   pop     de
 109++8B52 C1                   pop     bc
 110++8B53 F1                   pop     af
 111++8B54 C9                   ret
 112++8B55

   6++8E2C              ; Read-only: no writes, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or paging OUT.

  14++8E2C              ; read every root-directory sector and print each valid 8.3 entry.
  15++8E2C              ; IN: P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER contains the partition boot sector from probe.

   1++914A              ; ============================================================================
   2++914A              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++914A              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++914A              ; This is a DI-only bootstrap with a masked IM2 vector installer.
   5++914A              ; IM2/vector setup is allowed; EI and hardware page OUT remain forbidden.
   6++914A              ; ============================================================================
   7++914A              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++914A              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++914A              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++914A              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++914A              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++914A              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++914A
  14++914A              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++914A              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++914A              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++914A              ; The boot installer may establish the vector, mapping and handler ownership
  18++914A              ; tuple while DI remains asserted. Interrupts stay disabled until a separate
  19++914A              ; controlled source/acknowledge test validates live delivery and RETI.
  20++914A              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  21++914A              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  22++914A              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  23++914A              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7

 306++931E              ; Stage 2.15: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 307++931E              ; Hardware vector installation and IM selection are deliberately separate.
 308++931E              ; Entry contract: CPU has already pushed the interrupted PC; W1/P5 code and
 309++931E              ; W2/P2 stack remain mapped. The dispatcher may freely clobber all registers.

   1+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A000              ; Project127 ATM-native debug console, ported from Project126
   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.4
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.
   8+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   9+ A000              P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25 EQU 6
  10+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 80
  11+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS EQU 13
  12+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP EQU 12

  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C
  15+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE5 EQU #7A
  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F
  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80

  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0

  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done

 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:
 263+ A163
 264+ A163 F5                   push    af
 265+ A164 C5                   push    bc
 266+ A165 D5                   push    de
 267+ A166 E5                   push    hl
 268+ A167 CD 1B 70             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 302+ A1A3 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 303+ A1A6 3C                   inc     a
 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 305+ A1A9 DA BA A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 306+ A1AC AF                   xor     a
 307+ A1AD 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 308+ A1B0 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 309+ A1B3 3C                   inc     a
 310+ A1B4 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 311+ A1B7 C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 312+ A1BA              .p126_print_at_col_ok:
 313+ A1BA 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 314+ A1BD C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:
 316+ A1C0 CD 36 70             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 318+ A1C6 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 319+ A1C8 ED 79                out     (c),a
 320+ A1CA 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a
 322+ A1CE E1                   pop     hl
 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af
 326+ A1D2 C9                   ret
 327+ A1D3              .p126_print_at_denied:
 328+ A1D3 E1                   pop     hl
 329+ A1D4 D1                   pop     de
 330+ A1D5 C1                   pop     bc

 332+ A1D7 C9                   ret

 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.

 598+ A40C
 599+ A40C
 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2
 610+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA      EQU #C0
 611+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT     EQU #C6
 612+ A40C
 613+ A40C              p127_video_mode_selftest:
 614+ A40C                      ; Preserve representative character and attribute bytes from both
 615+ A40C                      ; parity planes. The mode changes themselves must not alter VRAM.
 616+ A40C AF                   xor     a
 617+ A40D 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 618+ A410 CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 619+ A413 D8                   ret     c
 620+ A414
 621+ A414 3E C3                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR
 622+ A416 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 623+ A419 D8                   ret     c
 624+ A41A CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 625+ A41D
 626+ A41D 3E C2                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640
 627+ A41F CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 628+ A422 D8                   ret     c
 629+ A423 CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 630+ A426
 631+ A426 3E C0                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA
 632+ A428 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 633+ A42B D8                   ret     c
 634+ A42C CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 635+ A42F
 636+ A42F                      ; Always return the controller to the Project127 UI mode.
 637+ A42F 3E C6                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
 638+ A431 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 639+ A434 D8                   ret     c
 640+ A435
 641+ A435 3E 01                ld      a,1
 642+ A437 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 643+ A43A CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 644+ A43D D8                   ret     c
 645+ A43E 3A C3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_even)
 646+ A441 21 C7 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_even
 647+ A444 BE                   cp      (hl)
 648+ A445 20 2D                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 649+ A447 3A C4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_odd)
 650+ A44A 21 C8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_odd
 651+ A44D BE                   cp      (hl)
 652+ A44E 20 24                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 653+ A450 3A C5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_even)
 654+ A453 21 C9 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_even
 655+ A456 BE                   cp      (hl)
 656+ A457 20 1B                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 657+ A459 3A C6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_odd)
 658+ A45C 21 CA A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd
 659+ A45F BE                   cp      (hl)
 660+ A460 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 661+ A462                      ; Compare complete-page CRCs: P7 character plane and P3 attributes.
 662+ A462 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 663+ A465 11 DC A5             ld      de,p127_video_crc_after
 664+ A468 06 04                ld      b,4
 665+ A46A              p127_video_crc_compare_loop:
 666+ A46A 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 667+ A46B BE                   cp      (hl)
 668+ A46C 20 06                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 669+ A46E 23                   inc     hl
 670+ A46F 13                   inc     de
 671+ A470 10 F8                djnz    p127_video_crc_compare_loop
 672+ A472 B7                   or      a
 673+ A473 C9                   ret
 674+ A474
 675+ A474              p127_video_test_fail:
 676+ A474 37                   scf
 677+ A475 C9                   ret
 678+ A476
 679+ A476              ; IN A=complete ATM mode-register byte. Carry means W3 UI lease refused.
 680+ A476              p127_video_switch_mode:
 681+ A476 32 C1 A5             ld      (p127_video_mode_arg),a
 682+ A479 C5                   push    bc
 683+ A47A D5                   push    de
 684+ A47B E5                   push    hl
 685+ A47C CD 1B 70             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 686+ A47F 38 6A                jr      c,p127_video_switch_refused
 687+ A481 CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 688+ A484
 689+ A484                      ; Preserve the original P3 bytes occupied by the temporary trampoline.
 690+ A484                      ; The trampoline returns to resident code, where these bytes are restored
 691+ A484                      ; before P3 is unmapped. This keeps the full-page CRC genuinely read-only.
 692+ A484 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 693+ A487 11 CB A5             ld      de,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 694+ A48A 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 695+ A48D ED B0                ldir
 696+ A48F
 697+ A48F                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p127_video_mode_return.
 698+ A48F 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 699+ A492 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 700+ A494 23                   inc     hl
 701+ A495 36 77                ld      (hl),#77
 702+ A497 23                   inc     hl
 703+ A498 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 704+ A49A 23                   inc     hl
 705+ A49B 36 ED                ld      (hl),#ED
 706+ A49D 23                   inc     hl
 707+ A49E 36 79                ld      (hl),#79
 708+ A4A0 23                   inc     hl
 709+ A4A1 36 C3                ld      (hl),#C3
 710+ A4A3 23                   inc     hl
 711+ A4A4 36 AF                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return & #FF
 712+ A4A6 23                   inc     hl
 713+ A4A7 36 A4                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return >> 8
 714+ A4A9 3A C1 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_mode_arg)
 715+ A4AC C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 716+ A4AF
 717+ A4AF              p127_video_mode_return:
 718+ A4AF                      ; P3 is still mapped here: restore the bytes overwritten by the trampoline.
 719+ A4AF 21 CB A5             ld      hl,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 720+ A4B2 11 EC FF             ld      de,#FFEC
 721+ A4B5 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 722+ A4B8 ED B0                ldir
 723+ A4BA CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 724+ A4BD CD 36 70             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 725+ A4C0
 726+ A4C0                      ; Verify the CPU windows and W3 ownership contract after EVERY mode
 727+ A4C0                      ; write. W2 must be the host page P5, W3 must be P0, and the UI lease
 728+ A4C0                      ; must be released before another mode transition is attempted.
 729+ A4C0 3A E4 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+1)
 730+ A4C3 FE 05                cp      5
 731+ A4C5 20 1F                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 732+ A4C7 3A E6 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 733+ A4CA B7                   or      a
 734+ A4CB 20 19                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 735+ A4CD 3A A8 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 736+ A4D0 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 737+ A4D2 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 738+ A4D4 3A A9 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 739+ A4D7 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 740+ A4D9 20 0B                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 741+ A4DB 3A AA 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 742+ A4DE B7                   or      a
 743+ A4DF 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 744+ A4E1 E1                   pop     hl
 745+ A4E2 D1                   pop     de
 746+ A4E3 C1                   pop     bc
 747+ A4E4 B7                   or      a
 748+ A4E5 C9                   ret
 749+ A4E6
 750+ A4E6              p127_video_mode_restore_error:
 751+ A4E6 E1                   pop     hl
 752+ A4E7 D1                   pop     de
 753+ A4E8 C1                   pop     bc
 754+ A4E9 37                   scf
 755+ A4EA C9                   ret
 756+ A4EB
 757+ A4EB              p127_video_switch_refused:
 758+ A4EB E1                   pop     hl
 759+ A4EC D1                   pop     de
 760+ A4ED C1                   pop     bc
 761+ A4EE 37                   scf
 762+ A4EF C9                   ret
 763+ A4F0
 764+ A4F0              ; Capture bytes from both character parity planes and both attribute planes.
 765+ A4F0              ; OUT: before signature when p127_video_capture_target=0, after when =1.
 766+ A4F0              p127_video_capture_signature:
 767+ A4F0 C5                   push    bc
 768+ A4F1 D5                   push    de
 769+ A4F2 E5                   push    hl

 774+ A4FE B7                   or      a
 775+ A4FF 20 1A                jr      nz,p127_video_capture_after
 776+ A501 3A C0 41             ld      a,(#41C0)

 795+ A533                      ; Full-page CRC-16/CCITT over the complete 16 KiB P7 and P3 windows.
 796+ A533                      ; This is read-only: no VRAM byte is overwritten by the test.

 803+ A541              p127_video_crc_store_after:

 805+ A544              p127_video_crc_store:

 807+ A547 77                   ld      (hl),a

 829+ A566 C9                   ret
 830+ A567
 831+ A567              ; Calculate CRC-16/CCITT (poly #1021, init #FFFF) over both mapped
 832+ A567              ; 16 KiB pages. W1=#4000..#7FFF is P7; W3=#C000..#FFFF is P3.
 833+ A567              ; OUT: p127_video_crc_p7 and p127_video_crc_p3, low byte first.
 834+ A567              p127_video_crc16_pages:
 835+ A567 C5                   push    bc
 836+ A568 D5                   push    de
 837+ A569 E5                   push    hl
 838+ A56A 21 00 40             ld      hl,#4000

 840+ A570 ED 53 D4 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p7),de
 841+ A574 21 00 C0             ld      hl,#C000
 842+ A577 CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 843+ A57A ED 53 D6 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p3),de

 845+ A57F D1                   pop     de
 846+ A580 C1                   pop     bc
 847+ A581 C9                   ret
 848+ A582
 849+ A582              ; IN HL=window base. OUT DE=CRC-16/CCITT.
 850+ A582              p127_video_crc16_page:
 851+ A582 11 FF FF             ld      de,#FFFF
 852+ A585 01 00 40             ld      bc,#4000
 853+ A588              p127_video_crc16_byte:
 854+ A588 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 855+ A589 AA                   xor     d
 856+ A58A 57                   ld      d,a
 857+ A58B 3E 08                ld      a,8
 858+ A58D 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 859+ A590              p127_video_crc16_bit:
 860+ A590 CB 23                sla     e
 861+ A592 CB 12                rl      d
 862+ A594 30 08                jr      nc,p127_video_crc16_no_poly
 863+ A596 7A                   ld      a,d
 864+ A597 EE 10                xor     #10
 865+ A599 57                   ld      d,a
 866+ A59A 7B                   ld      a,e
 867+ A59B EE 21                xor     #21
 868+ A59D 5F                   ld      e,a
 869+ A59E              p127_video_crc16_no_poly:
 870+ A59E 3A D3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_bits)
 871+ A5A1 3D                   dec     a
 872+ A5A2 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 873+ A5A5 20 E9                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_bit

 875+ A5A8 0B                   dec     bc
 876+ A5A9 78                   ld      a,b
 877+ A5AA B1                   or      c
 878+ A5AB 20 DB                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_byte
 879+ A5AD C9                   ret

 890+ A5B6              .p127_video_delay_inner:

   1+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A5E0              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A5E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A5E0              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A5E0              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A5E0              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A5E0
  15+ A5E0              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A5E0              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A5E0              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A5E0              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A5E0              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A5E0              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A5E0              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:

 241+ A7E5 CA B1 A8             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_duplicate
 242+ A7E8 23                   inc     hl
 243+ A7E9 10 F8                djnz    p127_physmem_duplicate_scan

 263+ A80F DA BB A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_reserve
 264+ A812
 265+ A812                      ; First sweep: fill every allocated physical page with a unique
 266+ A812                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.
 267+ A812 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 268+ A815 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 269+ A818 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 270+ A81B              p127_physmem_full_fill_loop:

 275+ A822 CD 94 AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_page

 287+ A83D 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 288+ A840 20 D9                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_fill_loop
 289+ A842
 290+ A842 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 291+ A844 CD 8E AC             call    p127_physmem_trace

 293+ A847                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A847                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A847 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A84A 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A84B 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A84D 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A850 19                   add     hl,de

 308+ A861 DA A2 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 309+ A864 3A 5D AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 310+ A867 EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 311+ A869 57                   ld      d,a

 314+ A86E E1                   pop     hl
 315+ A86F DA AC A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 316+ A872 3A 5E AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A875                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A875 B7                   or      a
 319+ A876 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A878 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A87B              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:
 322+ A87B 3D                   dec     a
 323+ A87C 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 324+ A87F 20 D0                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_verify_loop

 326+ A881 3E 56                ld      a,'V'

 328+ A886
 329+ A886                      ; Verify the allocator still marks every listed page ALLOCATED.

 372+ A8CA 3A 55 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)

 419+ A922 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A924              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A924                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A924 3A 51 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)

 438+ A94B              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:

 441+ A94F 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A951                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A951                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A951                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 447+ A955 C9                   ret

 451+ A958
 452+ A958              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A958              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A958              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A958                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A958                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A958                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A958                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.

 576+ AA94              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.

 646+ AAF3 C9                   ret
 647+ AAF4
 648+ AAF4              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ AAF4              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ AAF4              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ AAF4 21 F2 74             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ AAF7 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ AAF9 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ AAFB              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ AAFB 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ AAFC FE 00                cp      0
 657+ AAFE 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ AB00 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ AB02 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ AB04 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ AB06 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ AB08 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ AB0A 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ AB0C FE 04                cp      4
 665+ AB0E 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ AB10 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ AB12 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ AB14 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ AB16 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ AB18 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ AB19 B7                   or      a
 672+ AB1A 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ AB1C 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ AB1E              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ AB1E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ AB1F FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ AB21 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ AB23              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ AB23 23                   inc     hl
 680+ AB24 0C                   inc     c
 681+ AB25 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ AB27 B7                   or      a
 683+ AB28 C9                   ret
 684+ AB29              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ AB29 37                   scf
 686+ AB2A C9                   ret
 687+ AB2B
 688+ AB2B              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ AB2B              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ AB2B
 691+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 692+ AB2B              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ AB2B              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ AB2B              ; Public ABI:
 695+ AB2B              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ AB2B              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ AB2B              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ AB2B              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ AB2B              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ AB2B              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 702+ AB2B              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ AB2B C3 92 72             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ AB2E
 705+ AB2E              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ AB2E 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ AB31 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ AB34 B7                   or      a
 709+ AB35 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ AB38 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ AB3B 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ AB3C 3A E6 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ AB3F B8                   cp      b
 714+ AB40 CA E8 AB             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ AB43 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ AB46 C3 A8 72             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ AB49
 718+ AB49              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ AB49              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ AB49 F3                   di
 721+ AB4A 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ AB4D B7                   or      a
 723+ AB4E C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ AB51 3A E6 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ AB54 B7                   or      a
 726+ AB55 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ AB58 CD 3B 70             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ AB5B D8                   ret     c
 729+ AB5C 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ AB5E 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ AB61 CD 5A 70             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ AB64 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ AB66 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ AB68 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ AB6B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ AB6E 32 8A AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ AB71 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ AB73 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ AB76 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ AB79 B7                   or      a
 741+ AB7A C9                   ret
 742+ AB7B              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ AB7B CD 82 70             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ AB7E AF                   xor     a
 745+ AB7F 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ AB82 37                   scf
 747+ AB83 C9                   ret
 748+ AB84
 749+ AB84              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ AB84              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ AB84 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ AB87 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ AB8A FE 01                cp      1
 754+ AB8C C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ AB8F 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ AB92 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ AB94 D2 E8 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ AB97 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ AB98 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ AB9A 21 F2 74             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ AB9D 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ AB9E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ AB9F FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ ABA1 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ ABA4 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ ABA7 C3 14 AD             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ ABAA
 768+ ABAA              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ ABAA              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ ABAA              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ ABAA 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ ABAD FE 01                cp      1
 773+ ABAF C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ ABB2 AF                   xor     a
 775+ ABB3 CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ ABB6 D8                   ret     c
 777+ ABB7 3A 89 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ ABBA B7                   or      a
 779+ ABBB 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ ABBD 3A 8A AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ ABC0 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ ABC3              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ ABC3 3A 88 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ ABC6 B7                   or      a
 785+ ABC7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ ABC9 CD 6E 70             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ ABCC D8                   ret     c
 788+ ABCD AF                   xor     a
 789+ ABCE 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ ABD1              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ ABD1 3A 87 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ ABD4 B7                   or      a
 793+ ABD5 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ ABD7 CD 82 70             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ ABDA D8                   ret     c
 796+ ABDB AF                   xor     a
 797+ ABDC 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ ABDF              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ ABDF AF                   xor     a
 800+ ABE0 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ ABE3 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ ABE6 B7                   or      a
 803+ ABE7 C9                   ret
 804+ ABE8
 805+ ABE8              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ ABE8 37                   scf
 807+ ABE9 C9                   ret
 808+ ABEA
 809+ ABEA              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ ABEA              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ ABEA              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ ABEA AF                   xor     a
 813+ ABEB 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ ABEE 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ ABF1 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ ABF4 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ ABF7 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ ABF9 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ ABFC D2 84 AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ ABFF CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AC02 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AC05 32 8C AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AC08 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AC0B 32 8D AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AC0E CD 49 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AC11 DA 7E AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AC14                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AC14 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AC16 CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AC19 D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AC1C 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AC1F CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AC22 DA 7B AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AC25                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AC25 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AC28 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AC2B D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AC2E 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AC30 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AC33 CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AC36 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AC39 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AC3C 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AC3D 3A 8D AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AC40 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AC41 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AC44 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AC47 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AC4A DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AC4D                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AC4D CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AC50 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AC53 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AC54 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AC57 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AC58 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AC5B 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AC5C CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AC5F DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AC62 3A A8 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AC65 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AC67 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AC6A 3A A9 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AC6D FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AC6F C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AC72 3A E6 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AC75 B7                   or      a
 868+ AC76 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AC79 B7                   or      a
 870+ AC7A C9                   ret
 871+ AC7B
 872+ AC7B              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AC7B CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AC7E              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AC7E 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AC81 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AC84              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AC84 37                   scf
 879+ AC85 C9                   ret

 895+ AC94              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AC94              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AC94              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AC94              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AC94 F5                   push    af
 900+ AC95 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AC96 D5                   push    de
 902+ AC97 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AC98 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AC9A 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
